# Skills · D · part 131

750 entries, draft-article to draft-outreach. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **draft-article**: Draft a blog post, journalistic article or press-style summary from an eval's findings memo, for a general or specialist audience. Writes findings/&lt;run_id&gt;/article-&lt;audience&gt;.md and hands publishing to the blog skills. Use when the user says "write a blog post about the eval", "turn the findings into an article", "draft a piece for journalists", or "pitch this to a publication". ([danielrosehill/AI-Eval](https://github.com/danielrosehill/AI-Eval/tree/HEAD/skills/draft-article) · ★3)
- **draft-article**: Use only when the user asked, in this conversation, for an X Article draft they created to be finished in the browser's editor. Nothing is ever published. ([craftsamo/dotconfig](https://github.com/craftsamo/dotconfig/tree/HEAD/hermes/plugins/social/x-access/skills/draft-article) · ★1)
- **draft-article**: Discover the author's intended direction and material, structure a proposed Article Brief, or write an evidence-linked draft from an approved Brief. Use after topic analysis and before direction approval or full drafting, including when sources alone could support a generic article. Resolve author discovery before classifying remaining evidence needs; preserve permitted voice without inventing experience. Do not use to edit a final draft or publish. ([flexim-io/seo-writers](https://github.com/flexim-io/seo-writers/tree/HEAD/skills/draft-article) · ★1)
- **draft-article**: 從一本已讀完／已 ingest 的書或文章，讀使用者自己的畫線與註解，產出一篇**理性、低修飾的 「原子文章骨架草稿」**——先想清楚讀者（TA）、給 3 個 Hook，再把書的論點與使用者**全部**的 論據（逐條 annotation）乾淨陳述、展開舉例；句子層次的情緒/口吻潤飾留給使用者自己加。只要 使用者想把「讀過的東西＋自己的畫線/註解」變成一篇可以接著編輯的長文骨架，就用這個 skill。 觸發語包括 /draft-article、「把《X》寫成一篇文章」「幫我起一篇 &lt;書名&gt; 的草稿」「整理一下我讀的 &lt;書&gt; 的重點寫成介紹」「draft an article from my notes on X」。即使使用者沒說「草稿」兩字、只說 「幫我把我讀的 X 整理成一篇」也要觸發。不要用在：替既有草稿潤稿/定稿/加聲音（這只出理性骨架）、 社群貼文（未來的 draft-post）、把素材收進 KB（那是 /ingest）、或重抽風格側寫（那是 style 抽取）。 ([shosho-chang/nakama](https://github.com/shosho-chang/nakama/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/draft-article) · ★1)
- **draft-article**: Write the publish-ready long-form article (1500–2500 words) from the article plan, with inline code snippets and direct repo links. Use this AFTER article-plan when the user wants the final article text — this skill produces the actual prose, not the outline. ([Damecek/content-marketplace](https://github.com/Damecek/content-marketplace/tree/HEAD/plugins/linkedin-content/skills/draft-article))
- **draft-article**: Draft working article structure, section goals, evidence placement, and style sheet ([karstenheld3/IPPS](https://github.com/karstenheld3/IPPS/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/draft-article))
- **draft-article-title**: Brainstorm and evaluate X article title options for an author based on a Google Doc draft and an initial title idea. Uses the author's historical engagement data to score angles. Trigger when the user shares a Google Doc URL and wants title ideas, says "brainstorm titles", "help with an article title", "what should I call this", or "weigh this title against options". Always use this skill when a gdoc + title question appear together. ([warpdotdev/social-monitoring-agent-oss](https://github.com/warpdotdev/social-monitoring-agent-oss/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/draft-article-title) · ★4)
